test_T6_case_study_on_a_crafted_drop (test_card7_mechanism.FaceEffectModelTests.test_T6_case_study_on_a_crafted_drop)
The case-study path itself on the trained run: a node whose knockout result is forced right->wrong. ... /mnt/data/peeradon/h2snn-model/H2S-NN/src/utils/checkpointer.py:69: FutureWarning: You are using `torch.load` with `weights_only=False` (the current default value), which uses the default pickle module implicitly. It is possible to construct malicious pickle data which will execute arbitrary code during unpickling (See https://github.com/pytorch/pytorch/blob/main/SECURITY.md#untrusted-models for more details). In a future release, the default value for `weights_only` will be flipped to `True`. This limits the functions that could be executed during unpickling.
